import {
  Salon,
  Service,
  Staff,
  Booking,
  QueueEntry,
  Review,
  User,
  NotificationItem,
  AnalyticsSummary,
  AuthAccount,
  UserRoleMap,
} from '../src/types';
import { adminDb } from './firebaseAdmin';
import { buildQueueKey, buildSlotKey } from './keys';
import { businessToday, shiftBusinessDate } from './lib/businessTime';
import crypto from 'crypto';

export function buildSlotReservationKey(
  salonId: string,
  staffOrResourceId: string,
  baseDate: string,
  minute: number
): { key: string; date: string; minute: number } {
  const dayOffset = Math.floor(minute / 1440);
  const normalizedMinute = ((minute % 1440) + 1440) % 1440;
  const targetDate = shiftBusinessDate(baseDate, dayOffset);
  const key = `${salonId}:${staffOrResourceId}:${targetDate}:${normalizedMinute}`;
  return { key, date: targetDate, minute: normalizedMinute };
}

// Write-through cache map that persists to Firestore
class FirestoreBackedMap<K extends string, V> extends Map<K, V> {
  public collectionName: string;
  private readonly proxies = new Map<K, V>();
  private readonly timers = new Map<K, NodeJS.Timeout>();

  constructor(collectionName: string) {
    super();
    this.collectionName = collectionName;
  }

  async initialize() {
    try {
      const snap = await adminDb.collection(this.collectionName).get();
      for (const doc of snap.docs) {
        const data = doc.data();
        if (this.collectionName === 'notifications' && !Array.isArray(data)) {
          const items = Array.isArray((data as any)?.items) ? (data as any).items : [];
          super.set(doc.id as K, items as unknown as V);
        } else {
          super.set(doc.id as K, data as V);
        }
      }
      console.log(`[DB] Loaded ${snap.size} items from ${this.collectionName}`);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`[DB Error] Failed to load ${this.collectionName}:`, err);
      throw err;
    }
  }

  set(key: K, value: V): this {
    const existingTimer = this.timers.get(key);
    if (existingTimer) {
      clearTimeout(existingTimer);
      this.timers.delete(key);
    }
    super.set(key, value);
    this.proxies.delete(key);
    try {
      const cleanValue = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
      const payload = Array.isArray(cleanValue) ? { items: cleanValue } : cleanValue;
      adminDb.collection(this.collectionName).doc(key).set(payload).catch((err: any) => {
        console.error(`[Firestore Error] Failed to save ${this.collectionName}/${key}:`, err);
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`[Firestore Error] Failed to save ${this.collectionName}/${key}:`, err);
    }
    return this;
  }

  async setAsync(key: K, value: V): Promise<void> {
    const existingTimer = this.timers.get(key);
    if (existingTimer) {
      clearTimeout(existingTimer);
      this.timers.delete(key);
    }
    super.set(key, value);
    this.proxies.delete(key);
    try {
      const cleanValue = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
      const payload = Array.isArray(cleanValue) ? { items: cleanValue } : cleanValue;
      const firestorePromise = adminDb.collection(this.collectionName).doc(key).set(payload);
      await Promise.race([
        firestorePromise,
        new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1500)),
      ]).catch((err) => {
        console.warn(`[Firestore Warning] Save ${this.collectionName}/${key} backgrounded:`, err?.message || err);
      });
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn(`[Firestore Error] Save ${this.collectionName}/${key}:`, err?.message || err);
    }
  }

  delete(key: K): boolean {
    const existingTimer = this.timers.get(key);
    if (existingTimer) {
      clearTimeout(existingTimer);
      this.timers.delete(key);
    }
    const existed = super.delete(key);
    this.proxies.delete(key);
    if (existed) {
      adminDb.collection(this.collectionName).doc(key).delete().catch(err => {
        console.error(`[Firestore Error] Failed to delete ${this.collectionName}/${key}:`, err);
      });
    }
    return existed;
  }

  clear(): void {
    for (const timer of this.timers.values()) {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
    this.timers.clear();
    super.clear();
    this.proxies.clear();
    adminDb.collection(this.collectionName).get().then(snap => {
      const batch = adminDb.batch();
      snap.docs.forEach(doc => batch.delete(doc.ref));
      return batch.commit();
    }).catch(err => {
      console.error(`[Firestore Error] Failed to clear ${this.collectionName}:`, err);
    });
  }

  cache(key: K, value: V): void {
    const existingTimer = this.timers.get(key);
    if (existingTimer) {
      clearTimeout(existingTimer);
      this.timers.delete(key);
    }
    super.set(key, value);
    this.proxies.delete(key);
  }

  get(key: K): V | undefined {
    const value = super.get(key);
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return value;

    const existingProxy = this.proxies.get(key);
    if (existingProxy) return existingProxy;

    const seen = new WeakMap<object, object>();
    const persist = () => {
      const existingTimer = this.timers.get(key);
      if (existingTimer) {
        clearTimeout(existingTimer);
        this.timers.delete(key);
      }
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.timers.delete(key);
        try {
          if (!key || typeof key !== 'string') return;
          const cleanValue = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
          const payload = Array.isArray(cleanValue) ? { items: cleanValue } : cleanValue;
          adminDb.collection(this.collectionName).doc(key).set(payload).catch(err => {
            console.warn(`[Firestore Warning] Save ${this.collectionName}/${key} delayed:`, err?.message || err);
          });
        } catch (err: any) {
          console.warn(`[Firestore Warning] Persist error for ${this.collectionName}/${key}:`, err?.message || err);
        }
      }, 50);
      this.timers.set(key, timer);
    };
    const wrap = (target: any): any => {
      if (!target || typeof target !== 'object') return target;
      const cached = seen.get(target);
      if (cached) return cached;
      const proxy = new Proxy(target, {
        get(object, property, receiver) {
          return wrap(Reflect.get(object, property, receiver));
        },
        set(object, property, nextValue, receiver) {
          const result = Reflect.set(object, property, nextValue, receiver);
          persist();
          return result;
        },
        deleteProperty(object, property) {
          const result = Reflect.deleteProperty(object, property);
          persist();
          return result;
        },
      });
      seen.set(target, proxy);
      return proxy;
    };

    const proxy = wrap(value) as V;
    this.proxies.set(key, proxy);
    return proxy;
  }

  *values(): IterableIterator<V> {
    for (const key of super.keys()) {
      const value = this.get(key);
      if (value !== undefined) yield value;
    }
  }
}

// In-Memory Production-Grade Database with transactional consistency & atomic locks backed by Firestore
class QGoDatabase {
  public users: FirestoreBackedMap<string, User> = new FirestoreBackedMap('users');
  public salons: FirestoreBackedMap<string, Salon> = new FirestoreBackedMap('salons');
  public services: FirestoreBackedMap<string, Service> = new FirestoreBackedMap('services');
  public staff: FirestoreBackedMap<string, Staff> = new FirestoreBackedMap('staff');
  public bookings: FirestoreBackedMap<string, Booking> = new FirestoreBackedMap('bookings');
  public queueEntries: FirestoreBackedMap<string, QueueEntry> = new FirestoreBackedMap('queueEntries');
  public reviews: FirestoreBackedMap<string, Review> = new FirestoreBackedMap('reviews');
  public notifications: FirestoreBackedMap<string, NotificationItem[]> = new FirestoreBackedMap('notifications');
  public otpStore: FirestoreBackedMap<string, { otp: string; expiresAt: number; attempts: number }> = new FirestoreBackedMap('otpStore');
  public authAccounts: FirestoreBackedMap<string, AuthAccount> = new FirestoreBackedMap('authAccounts');
  public userRoles: FirestoreBackedMap<string, UserRoleMap> = new FirestoreBackedMap('userRoles');
  public supportTickets: FirestoreBackedMap<string, any> = new FirestoreBackedMap('supportTickets');
  public featureRequests: FirestoreBackedMap<string, any> = new FirestoreBackedMap('featureRequests');
  public featureRequestVotes: FirestoreBackedMap<string, any> = new FirestoreBackedMap('featureRequestVotes');
  public adminAuditLogs: FirestoreBackedMap<string, any> = new FirestoreBackedMap('adminAuditLogs');
  public paymentOrders: FirestoreBackedMap<string, any> = new FirestoreBackedMap('paymentOrders');

  // Concurrency Slot Lock Map (key: salonId:staffId:date:time)
  private slotLocks: Map<string, string> = new Map(); // Ephemeral, memory only

  // Queue sequence tracker (key: salonId:date -> counter)
  private queueSequence: Map<string, number> = new Map(); // Ephemeral, memory only

  async initializeAll() {
    console.log('[DB] Initializing Firestore collections...');
    await Promise.all([
      this.users.initialize(),
      this.salons.initialize(),
      this.services.initialize(),
      this.staff.initialize(),
      this.bookings.initialize(),
      this.queueEntries.initialize(),
      this.reviews.initialize(),
      this.notifications.initialize(),
      this.otpStore.initialize(),
      this.authAccounts.initialize(),
      this.userRoles.initialize(),
      this.supportTickets.initialize(),
      this.featureRequests.initialize(),
      this.featureRequestVotes.initialize(),
      this.adminAuditLogs.initialize(),
      this.paymentOrders.initialize(),
    ]);
    console.log('[DB] Initialization complete!');
  }

  // --- Transactions & Advanced Queries ---
  public getNextQueueNumber(salonId: string, date: string): number {
    const key = buildQueueKey(salonId, date);
    const next = (this.queueSequence.get(key) || 0) + 1;
    this.queueSequence.set(key, next);
    return next;
  }

  public getQueueCount(salonId: string, date: string): number {
    return Array.from(this.queueEntries.values()).filter(q => q.salonId === salonId && q.date === date).length;
  }

  public getWaitTimeEstimate(salonId: string, date: string): number {
    const queue = Array.from(this.queueEntries.values()).filter(q => q.salonId === salonId && q.date === date && q.status === 'waiting');
    return queue.length * 15; // 15 mins per person
  }

  public getLiveQueueState(salonId: string, date: string) {
    const queue = Array.from(this.queueEntries.values()).filter(q => q.salonId === salonId && q.date === date);
    const waiting = queue.filter(q => q.status === 'waiting');
    const serving = queue.filter(q => q.status === 'in_chair')[0];
    
    return {
      servingNow: serving ? serving.tokenNumber : null,
      totalWaiting: waiting.length,
      estimatedWaitTime: waiting.length * 15
    };
  }

  // --- Slot Locks ---

  public recalculateQueueStats(salonId: string): void {
    // No-op for now, frontend reads live state
  }

  public recalculateQueue(salonId: string): void {}



  public isSlotLocked(
    salonId: string,
    staffOrResourceId: string,
    date: string,
    time: string
  ): boolean {
    const key = buildSlotKey(salonId, staffOrResourceId, date, time);
    return this.slotLocks.has(key);
  }

  public getNextTokenNumber(salonOrId: any): string {
    const id = typeof salonOrId === 'string' ? salonOrId : salonOrId.id;
    const date = businessToday();
    const next = this.getNextQueueNumber(id, date);
    return `T${next.toString().padStart(3, '0')}`;
  }

  public getAnalytics(salonId: string): AnalyticsSummary {
    const bookingsList = Array.from(this.bookings.values()).filter(
      (b) => b.salonId === salonId
    );
    const today = businessToday();

    const todayBookings = bookingsList.filter(
      (b) => b.createdAt.startsWith(today) || b.scheduledDate === today
    );

    const completed = todayBookings.filter((b) => b.status === 'completed');
    const waiting = todayBookings.filter(
      (b) => b.status === 'confirmed' || b.status === 'checked_in'
    );
    const cancelled = todayBookings.filter((b) => b.status === 'cancelled');
    const noShow = todayBookings.filter((b) => b.status === 'no_show');

    const todayRevenue = completed.reduce((sum, b) => sum + (b.amount || 0), 0);

    return {
      todayBookingsCount: todayBookings.length,
      todayCompletedCount: completed.length,
      todayWaitingCount: waiting.length,
      todayCancelledCount: cancelled.length,
      todayNoShowCount: noShow.length,
      todayRevenue,
      averageWaitTimeMinutes: 0,
      hourlyTraffic: [],
      servicePopularity: [],
      staffPerformance: [],
    };
  }

  public tryAcquireSlotLock(salonId: string, staffId: string, date: string, time: string, customerId: string): boolean {
    const key = buildSlotKey(salonId, staffId, date, time);
    if (this.slotLocks.has(key)) {
      return false; // Already locked
    }
    this.slotLocks.set(key, customerId);
    // Auto-release lock after 5 minutes if booking fails
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.slotLocks.get(key) === customerId) {
        this.slotLocks.delete(key);
      }
    }, 5 * 60 * 1000);
    return true;
  }

  public releaseSlotLock(salonId: string, staffId: string, date: string, time: string, customerId?: string): void {
    const key = buildSlotKey(salonId, staffId, date, time);
    if (!customerId || this.slotLocks.get(key) === customerId) {
      this.slotLocks.delete(key);
    }
  }

  public async createBookingWithSlotReservation(
    booking: Booking,
    staffOrResourceId: string
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!booking.scheduledDate || !booking.scheduledTime) return false;

    const startMinutes = this.timeToMinutes(booking.scheduledTime);
    const endMinutes = booking.slotEndTime
      ? this.timeToMinutes(booking.slotEndTime)
      : startMinutes + booking.serviceDuration;
    const duration = endMinutes <= startMinutes ? endMinutes + 1440 - startMinutes : endMinutes - startMinutes;
    const slotStarts = Array.from(
      { length: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(duration / 15)) },
      (_, index) => startMinutes + index * 15
    );
    const reservationItems = slotStarts.map((minute) =>
      buildSlotReservationKey(booking.salonId, staffOrResourceId, booking.scheduledDate!, minute)
    );
    const reservationRefs = reservationItems.map((item) => {
      const reservationId = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(item.key).digest('hex');
      return adminDb.collection('slotReservations').doc(reservationId);
    });

    // 1. In-memory conflict check first
    const inMemoryConflict = Array.from(this.bookings.values()).some((b) => {
      if (b.salonId !== booking.salonId || b.scheduledDate !== booking.scheduledDate) return false;
      if (b.status === 'cancelled' || b.status === 'no_show') return false;
      const existingTargetId = b.resourceId || b.staffId;
      if (existingTargetId && existingTargetId !== staffOrResourceId) return false;
      const bStart = this.timeToMinutes(b.scheduledTime || '00:00');
      const bEnd = b.slotEndTime ? this.timeToMinutes(b.slotEndTime) : bStart + (b.serviceDuration || 60);
      const rStart = startMinutes;
      const rEnd = endMinutes;
      const bE = bEnd <= bStart ? bEnd + 1440 : bEnd;
      const rE = rEnd <= rStart ? rEnd + 1440 : rEnd;
      return rStart < bE && rE > bStart;
    });

    if (inMemoryConflict) return false;

    // 2. Commit to cache immediately
    this.bookings.cache(booking.id, booking);

    // 3. Attempt Firestore persistence without false-positive timeout
    try {
      const transactionSuccess = await adminDb.runTransaction(async (transaction) => {
        const existingReservations = await Promise.all(reservationRefs.map((reference) => transaction.get(reference)));
        if (existingReservations.some((reservation) => reservation.exists)) return false;

        transaction.set(adminDb.collection('bookings').doc(booking.id), booking);
        reservationRefs.forEach((reference, index) => {
          const item = reservationItems[index];
          transaction.set(reference, {
            bookingId: booking.id,
            salonId: booking.salonId,
            staffOrResourceId,
            date: item.date,
            minute: item.minute,
            createdAt: booking.createdAt,
          });
        });
        return true;
      });

      if (!transactionSuccess) {
        this.bookings.delete(booking.id);
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn(`[Firestore Quota/Network Warning] Save booking ${booking.id} backgrounded:`, err?.message || err);
      try {
        adminDb.collection('bookings').doc(booking.id).set(booking).catch(() => {});
      } catch {}
      return true;
    }
  }

  public releaseBookingSlotReservation(booking: Booking): void {
    const staffOrResourceId = booking.resourceId || booking.staffId;
    if (!staffOrResourceId || !booking.scheduledDate || !booking.scheduledTime) return;

    const startMinutes = this.timeToMinutes(booking.scheduledTime);
    const endMinutes = booking.slotEndTime
      ? this.timeToMinutes(booking.slotEndTime)
      : startMinutes + booking.serviceDuration;
    const duration = endMinutes <= startMinutes ? endMinutes + 1440 - startMinutes : endMinutes - startMinutes;
    const slotStarts = Array.from(
      { length: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(duration / 15)) },
      (_, index) => startMinutes + index * 15
    );
    const reservationItems = slotStarts.map((minute) =>
      buildSlotReservationKey(booking.salonId, staffOrResourceId, booking.scheduledDate!, minute)
    );

    void adminDb.runTransaction(async (transaction) => {
      const references = reservationItems.map((item) => {
        return adminDb.collection('slotReservations').doc(crypto.createHash('sha256').update(item.key).digest('hex'));
      });
      const reservations = await Promise.all(references.map((reference) => transaction.get(reference)));
      reservations.forEach((reservation, index) => {
        if (reservation.exists && reservation.data()?.bookingId === booking.id) {
          transaction.delete(references[index]);
        }
      });
    }).catch((error) => console.error('Failed to release booking slot reservation:', error));
  }

  private timeToMinutes(time: string): number {
    const [hours, minutes] = time.split(':').map(Number);
    return hours * 60 + minutes;
  }

  // Helper method to clear everything except demo data for testing
  public clearAll() {
    this.users.clear();
    this.salons.clear();
    this.services.clear();
    this.staff.clear();
    this.bookings.clear();
    this.queueEntries.clear();
    this.reviews.clear();
    this.notifications.clear();
    this.otpStore.clear();
    this.authAccounts.clear();
    this.userRoles.clear();
    this.paymentOrders.clear();
  }
}

export const db = new QGoDatabase();

/**
 * Standard JSON 429 Rate Limit Response Handler
 */
const rateLimitHandler = (req: Request, res: Response) => {
  res.status(429).json({ success: false, error: 'Too many requests, please try again later.' });
};

/**
 * 1. General API Limiter: 300 req / minute per IP
 * Configured high enough for client polling (~4-5s interval across views).
 */
export const generalApiLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000,
  max: 300,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  handler: rateLimitHandler,
  skip: (req: Request) => req.path === '/api/health' || req.path === '/health',
});

/**
 * 2. Strict Auth Limiter: 20 req / 15 minutes per IP
 * Protects login, registration, and OTP endpoints against brute-force.
 */
export const authLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
  max: 20,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  handler: rateLimitHandler,
  keyGenerator: (req: Request) => req.ip || req.socket.remoteAddress || 'unknown-ip',
  skip: (req: Request) =>
    req.method === 'GET' ||
    req.path === '/firebase/session' ||
    req.path.endsWith('/firebase/session') ||
    req.originalUrl?.includes('/auth/firebase/session'),
});

/**
 * 3. Dedicated OTP Request Limiter: 5 req / 15 minutes per IP
 * Strict limit on generating new SMS verification codes to prevent SMS pumping / exhaustion.
 */
export const otpRequestLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
  max: 5,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  handler: rateLimitHandler,
  keyGenerator: (req: Request) => req.ip || req.socket.remoteAddress || 'unknown-ip',
});

/**
 * 4. Ticket Verification / Check-in Limiter: 60 req / minute per IP
 * Prevents automated enumeration of QR signatures or short booking codes.
 */
export const checkinLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000,
  max: 60,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  handler: rateLimitHandler,
});

/**
 * 5. Write Limiter: 30 req / minute per IP
 * Throttles state-changing mutation requests (booking creation, queue joins, ticket creation).
 */
export const writeLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000,
  max: 30,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  handler: rateLimitHandler,
  skip: (req: Request) => req.method === 'GET' || req.method === 'HEAD' || req.method === 'OPTIONS',
});

/**
 * 6. Admin Limiter: 60 req / minute per IP
 * Protects administrative back-office queries and status mutations.
 */
export const adminLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000,
  max: 60,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  handler: rateLimitHandler,
});

/**
 * Clean 413 Payload Too Large Error Handler
 */
export function payloadTooLargeHandler(err: any, req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  if (err && (err.type === 'entity.too.large' || err.status === 413 || err.statusCode === 413)) {
    return res.status(413).json({ success: false, error: 'Payload too large' });
  }
  next(err);
}

/**
 * Global Production Error Handler
 * Sanitizes uncaught errors to prevent stack trace or file system path leakage.
 */
export function globalErrorHandler(err: any, req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  if (res.headersSent) {
    return next(err);
  }

  const statusCode = Number(err.status || err.statusCode) || 500;
  console.error(`[Server Error] ${req.method} ${req.originalUrl || req.url} -> ${statusCode}:`, err?.message || err);

  const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
  return res.status(statusCode).json({
    success: false,
    error: isProd
      ? 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again later.'
      : (err?.message || 'Internal server error'),
  });
}

==================== FILE: server\middleware\validation.ts ====================

import { z } from 'zod';
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';

export function validateBody<T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(schema: T) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const result = schema.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!result.success) {
      const firstIssue = result.error.issues[0];
      const fieldPath = firstIssue?.path?.join('.') || 'body';
      const message = firstIssue?.message || 'Invalid input data';
      return res.status(400).json({
        success: false,
        error: `Validation error on ${fieldPath}: ${message}`,
        code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR',
      });
    }
    // Replace req.body with parsed/sanitized data (strips unknown fields)
    req.body = result.data;
    next();
  };
}

==================== FILE: server\lib\businessTime.ts ====================

/**
 * Business Time Helpers
 *
 * All business logic dates/times must use these helpers so the server
 * produces consistent results regardless of the host's own timezone.
 *
 * The canonical timezone offset is read from QGO_TZ_OFFSET env var
 * (default: "+05:30" for IST). Every function accepts an injectable
 * `now` (epoch-ms) for deterministic testing.
 */

/** Offset string, e.g. "+05:30" or "-04:00". */
export const BUSINESS_TZ_OFFSET: string =
  process.env.QGO_TZ_OFFSET || '+05:30';

/**
 * Parse the offset string into total minutes from UTC.
 * "+05:30" → +330, "-04:00" → −240.
 */
function parseOffset(offset: string): number {
  const match = /^([+-])(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(offset);
  if (!match) throw new Error(`Invalid QGO_TZ_OFFSET: "${offset}"`);
  const sign = match[1] === '+' ? 1 : -1;
  return sign * (parseInt(match[2], 10) * 60 + parseInt(match[3], 10));
}

const offsetMinutes = parseOffset(BUSINESS_TZ_OFFSET);

/**
 * Return the current "YYYY-MM-DD" in business time.
 */
export function businessToday(now: number = Date.now()): string {
  // Shift the epoch by the offset, then extract UTC date parts.
  const shifted = new Date(now + offsetMinutes * 60_000);
  const y = shifted.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = String(shifted.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(shifted.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

/**
 * Return an absolute epoch-ms timestamp for a given date + "HH:mm" in
 * business time. This is the unambiguous instant that slot refers to.
 */
export function slotInstantMs(dateStr: string, time: string): number {
  // Build an ISO-like string with the explicit business offset.
  const iso = `${dateStr}T${time}:00${BUSINESS_TZ_OFFSET}`;
  const ms = Date.parse(iso);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid date/time: "${dateStr}" "${time}"`);
  }
  return ms;
}

/**
 * Current minute-of-day in business time (0–1439).
 */
export function businessMinutesOfDay(now: number = Date.now()): number {
  const shifted = new Date(now + offsetMinutes * 60_000);
  return shifted.getUTCHours() * 60 + shifted.getUTCMinutes();
}

/**
 * Day-of-week in business time (0 = Sun … 6 = Sat).
 */
export function businessDayOfWeek(now: number = Date.now()): number {
  const shifted = new Date(now + offsetMinutes * 60_000);
  return shifted.getUTCDay();
}

/**
 * Day-of-month in business time.
 */
export function businessDayOfMonth(now: number = Date.now()): number {
  const shifted = new Date(now + offsetMinutes * 60_000);
  return shifted.getUTCDate();
}

/**
 * Shift a "YYYY-MM-DD" date string by a given number of days.
 */
export function shiftBusinessDate(dateStr: string, days: number): string {
  if (days === 0) return dateStr;
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split('-').map(Number);
  const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d + days, 12, 0, 0));
  const newY = dt.getUTCFullYear();
  const newM = String(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const newD = String(dt.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${newY}-${newM}-${newD}`;
}

==================== FILE: server\lib\ownerTickets.ts ====================

import type { Booking } from '../../src/types';

export interface OwnerTicketEligibilityError {
  code: 'CANCELLED' | 'EXPIRED';
  message: string;
}

export function extractOwnerTicketId(payload: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof payload !== 'string' || payload.length > 2048) return null;
  const input = payload.trim();
  if (!input) return null;
  let candidate: unknown = input;

  if (input.startsWith('{')) {
    try {
      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(input);
      if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null || Array.isArray(parsed)) return null;
      const record = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
      candidate = record.ticketId || record.bookingId || record.id || record.qrCodeId || record.tokenNumber || record.token;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  } else if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(input) || input.includes('?')) {
    try {
      const url = new URL(input, 'https://qgo.app');
      candidate = url.searchParams.get('ticketId')
        || url.searchParams.get('bookingId')
        || url.searchParams.get('id')
        || url.searchParams.get('bId')
        || url.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop()
        || '';
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  if (typeof candidate !== 'string') return null;
  const cleaned = candidate.trim().replace(/^QGO-/i, '');
  return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(cleaned) ? cleaned : null;
}

export function findOwnerTicketBooking(payload: unknown, bookings: Iterable<Booking>): Booking | null {
  const cleanId = extractOwnerTicketId(payload);
  if (!cleanId) return null;
  const normalized = cleanId.toUpperCase();
  const allBookings = Array.from(bookings);
  const exactMatches = allBookings.filter((booking) => (
    booking.id.toUpperCase() === normalized
    || booking.id.replace(/^(bk_turf_|bk_walkin_|bk_|booking_)/i, '').toUpperCase() === normalized
    || (booking.qrCodeId || '').toUpperCase() === normalized
    || booking.tokenNumber?.toUpperCase() === normalized
  ));
  if (exactMatches.length === 1) return exactMatches[0];
  if (exactMatches.length > 1 || normalized.length > 8) return null;

  const legacyMatches = allBookings.filter((booking) => (
    (booking.id.split('_').pop() || '').toUpperCase() === normalized
  ));
  return legacyMatches.length === 1 ? legacyMatches[0] : null;
}

export function getBookingSlotId(booking: Booking): string {
  return booking.slotId || [
    booking.salonId,
    booking.resourceId || booking.staffId || 'default',
    booking.scheduledDate || '',
    booking.scheduledTime || '',
    booking.slotEndTime || '',
  ].join(':');
}

export function getOwnerTicketEligibilityError(
  booking: Booking,
  expired: boolean
): OwnerTicketEligibilityError | null {
  if (booking.status === 'cancelled') {
    return { code: 'CANCELLED', message: 'This booking was cancelled and cannot be completed.' };
  }
  if (booking.refundStatus === 'completed' || booking.refundAmount > 0 || /refund/i.test(booking.paymentStatus || '')) {
    return { code: 'CANCELLED', message: 'This booking has been refunded and cannot be completed.' };
  }
  if (booking.status === 'no_show' || expired) {
    return { code: 'EXPIRED', message: 'This ticket has expired and cannot be completed.' };
  }
  return null;
}

import {
  Salon,
  Service,
  Staff,
  Booking,
  QueueEntry,
  Review,
  User,
  NotificationItem,
  AnalyticsSummary,
  DashboardOverviewData,
  OwnerCustomerSummary,
  OwnerRevenueData,
  GeneratedTurfSlot,
  AdminOverviewResponse,
  AdminOwnerRecord,
  AdminCustomerRecord,
  AdminOwnersResponse,
  AdminCustomersResponse,
} from '../types';
import { storage } from '../lib/firebase';
import { ref, uploadBytes, getDownloadURL } from 'firebase/storage';
import { processImageForUpload } from '../utils/imageUpload';

import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';

// Scoped safe fetch wrapper for API methods to prevent SyntaxError on non-JSON HTTP error responses
// without globally mutating Response.prototype.
const rawFetch = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.fetch.bind(window) : globalThis.fetch.bind(globalThis);
const fetch = async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response> => {
  const res = await rawFetch(input, init);
  return new Proxy(res, {
    get(target, prop) {
      if (prop === 'json') {
        return async function () {
          try {
            const data = await target.json();
            if (!target.ok && typeof data === 'object' && data !== null && (data as any).success === undefined) {
              (data as any).success = false;
            }
            return data;
          } catch (err: any) {
            if (!target.ok) {
              return {
                success: false,
                error: `Server returned HTTP ${target.status} (${target.statusText || 'Error'}). Please check backend connection.`,
                code: `HTTP_${target.status}`,
                status: target.status,
              };
            }
            return {
              success: false,
              error: err?.message || 'Invalid server response.',
            };
          }
        };
      }
      const val = Reflect.get(target, prop, target);
      return typeof val === 'function' ? val.bind(target) : val;
    },
  });
};

// In web browsers (PC, mobile browser via DevTunnels or LAN), relative URLs ('') ensure API requests route to the current serving host.
// In the Capacitor Android APK, VITE_API_URL or custom override is used to connect to the external backend.
const rawApiUrl = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL as string | undefined)?.trim() || '';
const isNative = Capacitor.isNativePlatform();

export function getEffectiveApiOrigin(): string {
  if (!isNative) return '';
  const stored = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('qgo_custom_api_url')?.trim() : null;
  return (stored || rawApiUrl).replace(/\/+$/, '');
}

export let API_ORIGIN = getEffectiveApiOrigin();
export let API_BASE = `${API_ORIGIN}/api/v1`;
export let isApiConfigured = !isNative || Boolean(API_ORIGIN);

export function setCustomApiUrl(url: string): void {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    if (url && url.trim()) {
      localStorage.setItem('qgo_custom_api_url', url.trim());
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('qgo_custom_api_url');
    }
  }
  API_ORIGIN = getEffectiveApiOrigin();
  API_BASE = `${API_ORIGIN}/api/v1`;
  isApiConfigured = !isNative || Boolean(API_ORIGIN);
}

type FirebaseSessionResult = {
  success: boolean;
  user?: User;
  error?: string;
  code?: string;
  status?: number;
  retriable?: boolean;
};

function networkFailure(message: string, code: 'API_NOT_CONFIGURED' | 'NETWORK_UNAVAILABLE'): FirebaseSessionResult {
  return { success: false, error: message, code, status: 0, retriable: true };
}

export function resolveApiUrl(path: string): string {
  const origin = getEffectiveApiOrigin();
  const cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
  if (cleanPath.startsWith('/api/v1/')) {
    return `${origin}${cleanPath}`;
  }
  return `${origin}/api/v1${cleanPath}`;
}

export function resolveAssetUrl(path: string): string {
  if (!path || path.startsWith('http://') || path.startsWith('https://') || path.startsWith('data:') || path.startsWith('blob:')) {
    return path;
  }
  const origin = getEffectiveApiOrigin();
  const cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
  return `${origin}${cleanPath}`;
}

// Fail-fast guard: Inside Capacitor native app, VITE_API_URL or custom URL must be configured
if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform() && !API_ORIGIN) {
  const msg = '[QGo Configuration Error] VITE_API_URL is required when running inside the Capacitor Android APK, but it is currently empty. API calls will fail. Please set VITE_API_URL before building.';
  console.error(msg);
}

export const AUTH_TOKEN_KEY = 'qgo_token';
const INLINE_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES = 650 * 1024;

function fileToDataUrl(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => typeof reader.result === 'string'
      ? resolve(reader.result)
      : reject(new Error('Could not prepare the image for upload.'));
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Could not read the image for upload.'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

function authHeaders(includeJson = true): HeadersInit {
  const token = localStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
  return {
    ...(includeJson ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}),
    ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
  };
}

export const api = {
  // Auth
  async loginWithFirebase(
    idToken: string,
    requestedRole?: 'consumer' | 'owner' | 'admin'
  ): Promise<FirebaseSessionResult> {
    if (!isApiConfigured) {
      return networkFailure(
        'This Android build has no backend API URL. Configure VITE_API_URL with the HTTPS URL of the QGo backend, then rebuild the APK.',
        'API_NOT_CONFIGURED'
      );
    }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/firebase/session`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${idToken}` },
        body: JSON.stringify({ requestedRole }),
      });
      const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      const body = contentType.includes('application/json') ? await res.json() : null;
      if (!res.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: body?.error || `The QGo server returned HTTP ${res.status}.`,
          code: body?.code || `HTTP_${res.status}`,
          status: res.status,
          retriable: res.status >= 500,
        };
      }
      if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') {
        return { success: false, error: 'The QGo server returned an invalid session response.', code: 'INVALID_API_RESPONSE', status: res.status };
      }
      return { ...(body as FirebaseSessionResult), status: res.status };
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.warn('[QGo API] Firebase session request failed', {
        url: `${API_BASE}/auth/firebase/session`,
        message: e?.message,
      });
      return networkFailure(
        'Unable to reach the QGo server. Check your connection and try again.',
        'NETWORK_UNAVAILABLE'
      );
    }
  },

  async requestOtp(phone: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string; devOtp?: string }> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/otp/request`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ phone }),
      });
      return await res.json();
    } catch (e: any) {
      return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Network error requesting verification code' };
    }
  },

  async verifyOtp(
    phone: string,
    otp: string,
    name?: string,
    role?: 'consumer' | 'owner'
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; token?: string; user?: User; error?: string }> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/otp/verify`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ phone, otp, name, role }),
      });
      return await res.json();
    } catch (e: any) {
      return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Network error verifying code' };
    }
  },

  async getMe(token?: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; user?: User; error?: string }> {
    try {
      const authToken = token || localStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
      if (!authToken) return { success: false, error: 'No token' };
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/me`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${authToken}` },
      });
      return await res.json();
    } catch (e: any) {
      return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Network error' };
    }
  },

  async switchRole(role: 'consumer' | 'owner' | 'admin'): Promise<{ success: boolean; user?: User; error?: string }> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/switch-role`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ role }),
      });
      return await res.json();
    } catch (e: any) {
      return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Network error' };
    }
  },

  // Salons
  async getSalons(params?: { search?: string; category?: string; maxWait?: number }): Promise<{ salons: Salon[]; count: number }> {
    try {
      const query = new URLSearchParams();
      if (params?.search) query.set('search', params.search);
      if (params?.category && params.category !== 'all') query.set('category', params.category);
      if (params?.maxWait) query.set('maxWait', String(params.maxWait));

      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/salons?${query.toString()}`);
      return await res.json();
    } catch (e: any) {
      return { salons: [], count: 0 };
    }
  },

  async getSalonDetail(id: string): Promise<{
    salon: Salon;
    services: Service[];
    staff: Staff[];
    reviews: Review[];
    queue: { length: number; estimatedWaitMinutes: number; entries: QueueEntry[] };
  }> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/salons/${id}`, {
      headers: authHeaders(false),
    });
    return await res.json();
  },

  async getOwnerSalons(): Promise<{ salons: Salon[]; count: number }> {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/owner/salons`, {
      headers: authHeaders(false),
    });
    return await res.json();
  },

  async blockDate(salonId: string, data: { date?: string; dates?: string[]; reason?: string; forceCancelBookings?: boolean }): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; affectedBookings?: Booking[] }> {

  // Geocoding & Live Location

  // Availability (Salons & Turfs)
  async getAvailability(
    salonId: string,
    serviceIdOrOptions?: string | { serviceId?: string; staffId?: string; date?: string; resourceId?: string },
    staffId?: string,
    date?: string,
    resourceId?: string
  ): Promise<{ isOpen: boolean; message?: string; dayName: string; slots: GeneratedTurfSlot[]; resources: any[] }> {
    const query = new URLSearchParams();
    if (typeof serviceIdOrOptions === 'object' && serviceIdOrOptions !== null) {
      if (serviceIdOrOptions.serviceId) query.set('service_id', serviceIdOrOptions.serviceId);
      if (serviceIdOrOptions.staffId) query.set('staff_id', serviceIdOrOptions.staffId);
      if (serviceIdOrOptions.date) query.set('date', serviceIdOrOptions.date);
      if (serviceIdOrOptions.resourceId) query.set('resource_id', serviceIdOrOptions.resourceId);
    } else {
      if (typeof serviceIdOrOptions === 'string') query.set('service_id', serviceIdOrOptions);
      if (staffId) query.set('staff_id', staffId);
      if (date) query.set('date', date);
      if (resourceId) query.set('resource_id', resourceId);
    }

    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/salons/${salonId}/availability?${query.toString()}`);
    return await res.json();
  },

  // Bookings (Appointments, Turf Slots & Live Tokens)
  async createBooking(bookingData: {

    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/bookings`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(),
      body: JSON.stringify(bookingData),
    });
    return await res.json();
  },

  // QR Check-in & Ticket Verification
  async verifyOwnerTicket(ticketId: string): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    code?: string;
    message?: string;
    completedAt?: string;
    ticketId?: string;
    customerName?: string;
    venueName?: string;
    slotId?: string;
    slotDate?: string;
    slotTime?: string;
    currentStatus?: string;
  }> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/owner/tickets/verify`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ ticketId }),
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
      if (!res.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          code: data?.code || data?.reason || 'VERIFICATION_FAILED',
          message: data?.message || data?.error || (res.status === 401 ? 'Please sign in to verify tickets.' : `Server returned error ${res.status}`),
          completedAt: data?.completedAt,
          ticketId: data?.ticketId,
          customerName: data?.customerName,
          venueName: data?.venueName,
          slotId: data?.slotId,
          slotDate: data?.slotDate,
          slotTime: data?.slotTime,
          currentStatus: data?.currentStatus,
        };
      }
      return data || { success: false, message: 'Invalid response from server' };
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.warn('[QGo API] verifyOwnerTicket network error:', e);
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Could not connect to QGo server. Please check network connection.',
        code: 'NETWORK_ERROR',
      };
    }
  },

  async completeOwnerTicket(ticketId: string): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    code?: string;
    message?: string;
    completedAt?: string;
    ticketId?: string;
    customerName?: string;
    venueName?: string;
    slotId?: string;
    slotDate?: string;
    slotTime?: string;
    currentStatus?: string;
    error?: string;
  }> {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/owner/tickets/complete`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders(),
        body: JSON.stringify({ ticketId }),
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
      if (!res.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          code: data?.code || data?.reason || 'COMPLETION_FAILED',
          message: data?.message || data?.error || `Server returned error ${res.status}`,
          completedAt: data?.completedAt,
          ticketId: data?.ticketId,
          customerName: data?.customerName,
          venueName: data?.venueName,
          slotId: data?.slotId,
          slotDate: data?.slotDate,
          slotTime: data?.slotTime,
          currentStatus: data?.currentStatus,
        };
      }
      return data || { success: false, message: 'Invalid server response' };
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) console.warn('[QGo API] completeOwnerTicket network error:', e);
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Could not connect to QGo server. Please check network connection.',
        code: 'NETWORK_ERROR',
      };
    }
  },

        valid: false,

      };

  // Firebase Storage
  async uploadImage(
    file: File,
    folderPath = 'salons/general'
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; url?: string; error?: string }> {
    let processedFile: File | undefined;
    try {
      processedFile = await processImageForUpload(file);
      const useInlineFallback =
        import.meta.env.DEV &&
        typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
        ['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(window.location.hostname);

      if (useInlineFallback) {
        if (processedFile.size > INLINE_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES) {
          return { success: false, error: 'Please use an image under 650 KB.' };
        }
        return { success: true, url: await fileToDataUrl(processedFile) };
      }

      const cleanFolder = folderPath.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '');
      const storageRef = ref(storage, `${cleanFolder}/${processedFile.name}`);
      await uploadBytes(storageRef, processedFile, {
        contentType: processedFile.type,
      });
      const publicUrl = await getDownloadURL(storageRef);
      return { success: true, url: publicUrl };
    } catch (err: any) {
      // Firebase Storage can reject browser uploads when its CORS configuration
      // does not include the current development origin. Keep the owner flow
      // usable by storing a tightly size-limited, already-compressed image inline.
      // Cloud Storage remains the preferred path whenever it is available.
      try {
        if (!processedFile) {
          return { success: false, error: err?.message || 'Upload failed' };
        }
        if (processedFile.size > INLINE_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES) {
          return {
            success: false,
            error: 'Firebase Storage blocked this upload and the compressed image is too large for fallback storage. Please use an image under 650 KB.',
          };
        }
        return { success: true, url: await fileToDataUrl(processedFile) };
      } catch (fallbackErr: any) {
        return { success: false, error: fallbackErr?.message || err?.message || 'Upload failed' };
      }
    }
  },
