==================== FILE: src\context\AuthContext.tsx ====================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useRef, useTransition } from 'react';
import { User, UserRole, Language } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../i18n';
import { api, AUTH_TOKEN_KEY } from '../services/api';
import { auth, firebaseAuthReady, isFirebaseConfigured } from '../lib/firebase';
import { Capacitor } from '@capacitor/core';
import {
  ConfirmationResult,
  onIdTokenChanged,
  RecaptchaVerifier,
  signInWithCustomToken,
  signInWithPhoneNumber,
  signOut,
} from 'firebase/auth';
interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  role: UserRole;
  currentRole: UserRole;
  language: Language;
  t: typeof translations['en'];
  setLanguage: (lang: Language) => void;
  setUser: (user: User | null) => void;
  loginWithGoogle: (
    credential: string,
    role: string,
    accessToken?: string,
    _name?: string,
    _picture?: string
  ) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string; code?: string }>;
  loginWithOtp: (
    phone: string,
    otp: string,
    name?: string,
    requestedRole?: 'consumer' | 'owner' | 'admin'
  ) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  requestOtp: (phone: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string; devOtp?: string }>;
  switchRole: (role: 'consumer' | 'owner' | 'admin') => Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
  logout: () => void;
  isAuthModalOpen: boolean;
  setIsAuthModalOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  isLoadingSession: boolean;
  sessionError: string | null;
  retrySession: () => void;
}

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const STORAGE_KEY = 'qgo_user';
const TOKEN_KEY = AUTH_TOKEN_KEY;
const LANG_KEY = 'qgo_lang';

function getStoredPreferredRole(): 'consumer' | 'owner' | 'admin' | undefined {
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!stored) return undefined;
    const user = JSON.parse(stored) as Pick<User, 'role' | 'activeRole' | 'email'>;
    if (user.email?.toLowerCase() === 'cjkishore35@gmail.com' || user.activeRole === 'admin' || user.role === 'admin') {
      return 'admin';
    }
    return user.activeRole === 'owner' || user.role === 'owner' ? 'owner' : 'consumer';
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
}

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  // Cached profile data is not authorization. A user is exposed to the app only
  // after the backend has verified a Firebase token for this launch.
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);

  const [isLoadingSession, setIsLoadingSession] = useState(true);
  const [sessionError, setSessionError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sessionRetryNonce, setSessionRetryNonce] = useState(0);

  const [language, setLanguageState] = useState<Language>(() => {
    return (localStorage.getItem(LANG_KEY) as Language) || (localStorage.getItem('qgo_language') as Language) || 'en';
  });

  const [isAuthModalOpen, setIsAuthModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const phoneConfirmation = useRef<ConfirmationResult | null>(null);
  const recaptchaVerifier = useRef<RecaptchaVerifier | null>(null);
  const nativeVerificationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const nativeAutoVerifiedRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const retryCountRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const inFlightSessionRef = useRef<Promise<{ success: boolean; user?: User; error?: string; code?: string }> | null>(null);
  const isServerOtpRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  const setLanguage = (lang: Language) => {
    setLanguageState(lang);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(LANG_KEY, lang);
      localStorage.setItem('qgo_language', lang);
    } catch {}
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const isNativeAuth = Capacitor.isNativePlatform();
    if (!isFirebaseConfigured && !isNativeAuth) {
      setIsLoadingSession(false);
      return;
    }

    // Native Firebase Authentication maintains its own session; it is not
    // mirrored into the Firebase Web SDK used by the Capacitor WebView. Restore
    // that native session explicitly so an app restart cannot strand a signed-in
    // user on the login screen just because localStorage was cleared.
    if (isNativeAuth) {
      let isMounted = true;
      const restoreNativeSession = async () => {
        try {
          const { FirebaseAuthentication } = await import('@capacitor-firebase/authentication');
          const { user: nativeUser } = await FirebaseAuthentication.getCurrentUser().catch(() => ({ user: null }));
          if (!nativeUser) {
            // Check if there is an existing session stored locally (e.g. OTP, phone, or local token)
            const existingToken = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
            const existingUserStr = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
            if (existingToken && existingUserStr) {
              try {
                const meRes = await api.loginWithFirebase(existingToken, getStoredPreferredRole());
                if (meRes.success && meRes.user && isMounted) {
                  setSessionError(null);
                  setUser(meRes.user);
                  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(meRes.user));
                  return;
                }
              } catch (err) {
                console.warn('[QGo Auth] Reconnection notice for native session:', err);
              }
              try {
                const cachedUser = JSON.parse(existingUserStr);
                if (cachedUser && cachedUser.id && isMounted) {
                  setUser(cachedUser);
                  return;
                }
              } catch {}
            }
            if (isMounted) {
              setUser(null);
            }
            return;
          }

          const { token } = await FirebaseAuthentication.getIdToken({ forceRefresh: false });
          const res = await api.loginWithFirebase(token, getStoredPreferredRole());
          if (res.success && res.user && isMounted) {
            setSessionError(null);
            localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token);
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.user));
            setUser(res.user);
          } else if (isMounted) {
            console.warn('[QGo Auth] Native session was rejected by the backend:', res.code, res.error);
            const isAuthRejected =
              res.status === 401 ||
              res.status === 403 ||
              res.code === 'HTTP_401' ||
              res.code === 'HTTP_403' ||
              res.code === 'OWNER_ACCESS_DENIED';

            if (isAuthRejected) {
              setUser(null);
              localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
              localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
              await FirebaseAuthentication.signOut().catch(() => undefined);
            } else {
              const cached = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
              if (cached) {
                try {
                  setUser(JSON.parse(cached));
                } catch {}
              }
              setSessionError(res.error || 'Reconnecting to QGo server...');
            }
          }
        } catch (error) {
          console.warn('[QGo Auth] Native session restore notice:', error);
          if (isMounted) {
            const cached = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
            if (cached) {
              try {
                setUser(JSON.parse(cached));
                return;
              } catch {}
            }
            setUser(null);
          }
        } finally {
          if (isMounted) setIsLoadingSession(false);
        }
      };
      void restoreNativeSession();
      return () => {
        isMounted = false;
      };
    }

    // Safety: always resolve loading within 2s regardless of Firebase state.
    const safetyTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      console.log('[QGo Auth] Safety timer expired - resolving loading state');
      setIsLoadingSession(false);
    }, 2000);

    let isMounted = true;

    // Handle mobile browser redirect flow if returning from Google OAuth
    if (!isNativeAuth && isFirebaseConfigured) {
      void import('firebase/auth')
        .then(({ getRedirectResult }) => {
          getRedirectResult(auth)
            .then(async (redirectResult) => {
              if (redirectResult && redirectResult.user && isMounted) {
                const idToken = await redirectResult.user.getIdToken();
                const pendingRole = (localStorage.getItem('qgo_pending_role') as any) || getStoredPreferredRole();
                localStorage.removeItem('qgo_pending_role');
                const res = await api.loginWithFirebase(idToken, pendingRole);
                if (res.success && res.user && isMounted) {
                  setSessionError(null);
                  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, idToken);
                  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.user));
                  startTransition(() => {
                    setUser(res.user!);
                  });
                  setIsLoadingSession(false);
                }
              }
            })
            .catch((err) => {
              console.warn('[QGo Auth] getRedirectResult notice:', err);
            });
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }

    const unsubscribe = onIdTokenChanged(auth, async (firebaseUser) => {
      try {
        if (!isMounted) return;

        // Wait for persistence to be configured, but don't let it block forever.
        await Promise.race([
          firebaseAuthReady,
          new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1500)),
        ]);

        if (!firebaseUser) {
          if (isMounted) {
            const existingToken = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY);
            const existingUserStr = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

            // If we have an existing local session (e.g. OTP login, Google OAuth session, or cached user),
            // validate it against the backend instead of wiping it out on page reload.
            if (existingToken && existingUserStr) {
              try {
                // Keep the role selected at sign-in when a development server restart
                // recreates the in-memory auth record. A plain /auth/me call would
                // otherwise recreate that account as a consumer.
                const meRes = await api.loginWithFirebase(existingToken, getStoredPreferredRole());
                if (meRes.success && meRes.user && isMounted) {
                  setSessionError(null);
                  setUser(meRes.user);
                  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(meRes.user));
                  setIsLoadingSession(false);
                  return;
                }
                if (meRes.retriable && isMounted) {
                  setSessionError(meRes.error || 'Unable to verify your session with the QGo server.');
                  setIsLoadingSession(false);
                  return;
                }
              } catch (meErr) {
                console.warn('[QGo Auth] Existing session check notice:', meErr);
                // On temporary network errors or offline, preserve the user from storage
                try {
                  const parsed = JSON.parse(existingUserStr);
                  if (parsed && parsed.id && isMounted) {
                    setUser(parsed);
                    setIsLoadingSession(false);
                    return;
                  }
                } catch {}
              }
            }

            // Only clear state if there is no existing valid local token
            setUser(null);
            localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
            setIsLoadingSession(false);
          }
          return;
        }

        let tokenToVerify: string | null = null;
        try {
          tokenToVerify = await firebaseUser.getIdToken();
          if (tokenToVerify) {
            localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, tokenToVerify);
          }
        } catch (tokErr) {
          console.warn('[QGo Auth] Error retrieving id token:', tokErr);
          if (isMounted) setUser(null);
          return;
        }

        if (!tokenToVerify) {
          if (isMounted) setUser(null);
          return;
        }

        // Single-flight deduplication: reuse in-flight session promise to avoid concurrent 401s
        if (!inFlightSessionRef.current) {
          inFlightSessionRef.current = api.loginWithFirebase(tokenToVerify, getStoredPreferredRole());
        }
        const res = await inFlightSessionRef.current;
        inFlightSessionRef.current = null;

        if (res.success && res.user && isMounted) {
          setSessionError(null);
          startTransition(() => {
            setUser(res.user!);
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.user));
          });
        } else if (isMounted) {
          console.warn('[QGo Auth] Session verification failed on server:', res.error, res.code, res.status);
          const isAuthRejected =
            res.status === 401 ||
            res.status === 403 ||
            res.code === 'HTTP_401' ||
            res.code === 'HTTP_403' ||
            res.code === 'auth/missing-token' ||
            res.code === 'auth/invalid-token' ||
            res.code === 'auth/id-token-expired' ||
            res.code === 'auth/id-token-revoked' ||
            res.code === 'OWNER_ACCESS_DENIED';

          if (isAuthRejected) {
            setUser(null);
            localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
            setSessionError(null);
            try {
              await signOut(auth);
            } catch {}
          } else {
            // On 429, 5xx, or network failure:
            // KEEP the Firebase session and cached profile, show non-destructive message, retry with backoff
            const cachedUserStr = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
            if (cachedUserStr) {
              try {
                setUser(JSON.parse(cachedUserStr));
              } catch {}
            }
            const isRateLimited = res.status === 429 || res.code === 'HTTP_429';
            const msg = isRateLimited
              ? 'High traffic detected. Retrying connection...'
              : (res.error || 'Unable to connect to QGo server. Retrying in background...');
            setSessionError(msg);

            const delay = Math.min(1000 * Math.pow(2, retryCountRef.current || 0), 10000);
            retryCountRef.current = (retryCountRef.current || 0) + 1;
            setTimeout(() => {
              if (isMounted) setSessionRetryNonce((n) => n + 1);
            }, delay);
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        inFlightSessionRef.current = null;
        console.error('[QGo Auth] Session validation error:', err);
      } finally {
        clearTimeout(safetyTimer);
        if (isMounted) {
          setIsLoadingSession(false);
        }
      }
    });

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      clearTimeout(safetyTimer);
      unsubscribe();
    };
  }, [sessionRetryNonce]);

  // Removed destructive useEffect[user]

  const loginWithGoogle = async (
    credential: string,
    requestedRole: string,
    accessToken?: string,
    _name?: string,
    _picture?: string
  ) => {
    try {
      const tokenToSend = credential || accessToken;
      if (!tokenToSend) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Google did not return a verifiable Firebase session.' };
      }
      const roleToUse = requestedRole === 'owner' ? 'owner' : 'consumer';
      // Execute role-aware session exchange
      const sessionPromise = api.loginWithFirebase(tokenToSend, roleToUse);
      inFlightSessionRef.current = sessionPromise;
      const res = await sessionPromise;
      inFlightSessionRef.current = null;

      if (res.success && res.user) {
        setSessionError(null);
        localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, tokenToSend);
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.user));
        startTransition(() => {
          setUser(res.user!);
        });
        setIsAuthModalOpen(false);
        return { success: true };
      }
      return { success: false, error: res.error || 'Authentication failed', code: res.code };
    } catch (e: any) {
      inFlightSessionRef.current = null;
      console.error('Google login error:', e);
      return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Network error during authentication' };
    }
  };

  const loginWithOtp = async (
    _phone: string,
    otp: string,
    _name?: string,
    requestedRole?: 'consumer' | 'owner'
  ) => {
    try {
      const code = otp.trim();
      if (!code) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Verification code is required.' };
      }

      let formattedPhone = _phone.replace(/[\s\-()]/g, '');
      if (formattedPhone.length === 10 && /^\d+$/.test(formattedPhone)) {
        formattedPhone = '+91' + formattedPhone;
      } else if (formattedPhone.length > 10 && /^\d+$/.test(formattedPhone) && !formattedPhone.startsWith('+')) {
        formattedPhone = '+' + formattedPhone;
      }

      let idToken: string | null = null;

      if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform() && !isServerOtpRef.current) {
        const { FirebaseAuthentication } = await import('@capacitor-firebase/authentication');
        if (nativeAutoVerifiedRef.current) {
          const tokenRes = await FirebaseAuthentication.getIdToken({ forceRefresh: false });
          idToken = tokenRes.token;
        } else if (nativeVerificationIdRef.current) {
          const confirmRes = await FirebaseAuthentication.confirmVerificationCode({
            verificationId: nativeVerificationIdRef.current,
            verificationCode: code,
          });
          idToken = confirmRes.credential?.idToken || null;
          if (!idToken) {
            const tokenRes = await FirebaseAuthentication.getIdToken({ forceRefresh: false });
            idToken = tokenRes.token;
          }
        }
      } else if (!isServerOtpRef.current && phoneConfirmation.current) {
        try {
          const userCred = await phoneConfirmation.current.confirm(code);
          idToken = await userCred.user.getIdToken();
        } catch (credErr) {
          console.warn('[QGo Auth] Firebase confirmation failed, attempting backend verify:', credErr);
        }
      }

      // If we don't have a Firebase ID token yet (or server-side OTP was used), verify with the backend
      if (!idToken) {
        const verifyRes = await api.verifyOtp(formattedPhone, code, _name, requestedRole);
        if (!verifyRes.success || !verifyRes.token) {
          return { success: false, error: verifyRes.error || 'Invalid or expired verification code.' };
        }

        // If the backend generated a custom token, exchange it via Firebase SDK for an ID token
        const customToken = verifyRes.token;
        if (auth && !Capacitor.isNativePlatform() && isFirebaseConfigured) {
          try {
            const userCred = await signInWithCustomToken(auth, customToken);
            idToken = await userCred.user.getIdToken();
          } catch (exchangeErr) {
            console.warn('[QGo Auth] signInWithCustomToken exchange note:', exchangeErr);
            idToken = customToken;
          }
        } else {
          idToken = customToken;
        }

        // If backend already returned verified user and idToken, complete sign-in directly
        if (verifyRes.user) {
          if (!inFlightSessionRef.current) {
            inFlightSessionRef.current = api.loginWithFirebase(idToken, requestedRole);
          }
          const res = await inFlightSessionRef.current;
          inFlightSessionRef.current = null;
          const finalUser = (res.success && res.user) ? res.user : verifyRes.user;
          setSessionError(null);
          localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, idToken);
          localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(finalUser));
          startTransition(() => {
            setUser(finalUser);
          });
          setIsAuthModalOpen(false);
          return { success: true };
        }
      }

      if (!idToken) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Could not obtain verification credentials.' };
      }

      if (!inFlightSessionRef.current) {
        inFlightSessionRef.current = api.loginWithFirebase(idToken, requestedRole);
      }
      const res = await inFlightSessionRef.current;
      inFlightSessionRef.current = null;
      if (res.success && res.user) {
        setSessionError(null);
        localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, idToken);
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.user));
        startTransition(() => {
          setUser(res.user!);
        });
        setIsAuthModalOpen(false);
        return { success: true };
      }
      return { success: false, error: res.error || 'OTP verification failed on server' };
    } catch (e: any) {
      inFlightSessionRef.current = null;
      console.error('OTP login error:', e);
      const code = e?.code || '';
      let msg = e?.message || 'Verification error. Please try again.';
      if (code === 'auth/invalid-verification-code') {
        msg = 'Invalid verification code. Please check and try again.';
      } else if (code === 'auth/code-expired') {
        msg = 'Verification code has expired. Please request a new code.';
      } else if (code === 'auth/session-expired') {
        msg = 'Verification session has expired. Please request a new code.';
      }
      return { success: false, error: msg };
    }
  };

  const requestOtp = async (phone: string) => {
    try {
      let formattedPhone = phone.replace(/[\s\-()]/g, '');
      if (formattedPhone.length === 10 && /^\d+$/.test(formattedPhone)) {
        formattedPhone = '+91' + formattedPhone;
      } else if (formattedPhone.length > 10 && /^\d+$/.test(formattedPhone) && !formattedPhone.startsWith('+')) {
        formattedPhone = '+' + formattedPhone;
      }

      if (!/^\+[1-9]\d{7,14}$/.test(formattedPhone)) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Enter your phone number with country code, for example +919876543210.' };
      }

      if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
        try {
          const { FirebaseAuthentication } = await import('@capacitor-firebase/authentication');
          nativeVerificationIdRef.current = null;
          nativeAutoVerifiedRef.current = false;
          isServerOtpRef.current = false;

          const codeSentPromise = new Promise<{ verificationId: string }>(async (resolve, reject) => {
            let timeoutTimer: number;
            let codeSentSub: any = null;
            let completedSub: any = null;
            let failSub: any = null;

            const cleanup = () => {
              clearTimeout(timeoutTimer);
              try {
                if (codeSentSub?.remove) void codeSentSub.remove();
                if (completedSub?.remove) void completedSub.remove();
                if (failSub?.remove) void failSub.remove();
              } catch {}
            };

            timeoutTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
              cleanup();
              reject(new Error('SMS verification request timed out. Please check signal and try again.'));
            }, 60000);

            try {
              codeSentSub = await FirebaseAuthentication.addListener('phoneCodeSent', (event) => {
                cleanup();
                resolve({ verificationId: event.verificationId });
              });

              completedSub = await FirebaseAuthentication.addListener('phoneVerificationCompleted', () => {
                cleanup();
                nativeAutoVerifiedRef.current = true;
                resolve({ verificationId: 'auto-verified' });
              });

              failSub = await FirebaseAuthentication.addListener('phoneVerificationFailed', (event: any) => {
                cleanup();
                const msg = event?.message || event?.error || 'Firebase phone verification failed on device.';
                reject(new Error(msg));
              });

              await FirebaseAuthentication.signInWithPhoneNumber({ phoneNumber: formattedPhone });
            } catch (initErr: any) {
              cleanup();
              reject(initErr);
            }
          });
          const res = await codeSentPromise;
          nativeVerificationIdRef.current = res.verificationId;
          return { success: true };
        } catch (nativeErr: any) {
          console.warn('[QGo Auth] Native phone auth failed, falling back to server OTP:', nativeErr?.message || nativeErr);
          nativeVerificationIdRef.current = null;
          // Fall through to server OTP
        }
      }

      // Web flow: check if recaptcha container is in DOM and try Firebase client SDK
      const recaptchaEl = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.getElementById('qgo-phone-recaptcha') : null;
      if (isFirebaseConfigured && recaptchaEl) {
        try {
          if (!recaptchaVerifier.current) {
            recaptchaVerifier.current = new RecaptchaVerifier(auth, 'qgo-phone-recaptcha', {
              size: 'invisible',
            });
          }
          const confirmation = await signInWithPhoneNumber(auth, formattedPhone, recaptchaVerifier.current);
          phoneConfirmation.current = confirmation;
          isServerOtpRef.current = false;
          return { success: true };
        } catch (firebaseErr: any) {
          console.warn('[QGo Auth] Firebase Web Phone Auth attempt notice, falling back to server OTP:', firebaseErr?.message);
          try {
            if (recaptchaVerifier.current?.clear) {
              recaptchaVerifier.current.clear();
            }
          } catch {}
          recaptchaVerifier.current = null;
        }
      }

      // Backend OTP service (reliable on web, dev environments, and when Firebase phone SMS quota is reached)
      const res = await api.requestOtp(formattedPhone);
      if (res.success) {
        isServerOtpRef.current = true;
        phoneConfirmation.current = null;
        return { success: true, devOtp: res.devOtp };
      }

      return { success: false, error: res.error || 'Unable to send verification code. Please try again.' };
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.warn('[QGo Auth] requestOtp error:', error);
      let msg = error?.message || 'Unable to send the verification code.';
      const code = error?.code || '';
      if (code === 'auth/invalid-phone-number') {
        msg = 'Invalid phone number format. Please check and try again.';
      } else if (code === 'auth/quota-exceeded') {
        msg = 'SMS quota for this project has been exceeded. Please use Google Sign-In or contact support.';
      } else if (code === 'auth/too-many-requests') {
        msg = 'Too many requests. Please wait a few minutes before trying again.';
      } else if (code === 'auth/captcha-check-failed') {
        msg = 'reCAPTCHA verification failed. Please try again.';
      }
      return { success: false, error: msg };
    }
  };

  const switchRole = async (nextRole: 'consumer' | 'owner' | 'admin') => {
    try {
      const res = await api.switchRole(nextRole);
      if (res.success && res.user) {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.user));
        startTransition(() => {
          setUser(res.user!);
        });
        return { success: true };
      }
      return { success: false, error: res.error || 'Role switch failed' };
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('Role switch error:', e);
      return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Network error during role switch' };
    }
  };

  const logout = () => {
    setUser(null);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY);
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_app_page');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_cv');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_sbid');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_venue');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_bs');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_booking');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_customer_tab');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_owner_tab');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_owner_wizard');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_owner_salon');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_wizard_step');
    sessionStorage.removeItem('qgo_admin_tab');
    if (auth && auth.currentUser) {
      signOut(auth).catch(() => {});
    }
    if (Capacitor.isNativePlatform()) {
      void import('@capacitor-firebase/authentication')
        .then(({ FirebaseAuthentication }) => FirebaseAuthentication.signOut())
        .catch((error) => console.warn('[QGo Auth] Native sign-out failed:', error));
    }
  };

  const retrySession = () => {
    setSessionError(null);
    setIsLoadingSession(true);
    setSessionRetryNonce((nonce) => nonce + 1);
  };

  const currentT = translations[language] || translations['en'];
  const isOwnerUrl = typeof window !== 'undefined' && (/^\/(owner|dashboard)/i.test(window.location.pathname));
  const isAdminUrl = typeof window !== 'undefined' && (/^\/admin/i.test(window.location.pathname));
  const storedRole = getStoredPreferredRole();
  const isSuperAdminUser = user?.email?.toLowerCase() === 'cjkishore35@gmail.com' || user?.role === 'admin' || storedRole === 'admin';
  const fallbackRole: UserRole = isSuperAdminUser ? 'admin' : (isOwnerUrl ? 'owner' : isAdminUrl ? 'admin' : (storedRole || 'consumer'));

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        role: (user?.email?.toLowerCase() === 'cjkishore35@gmail.com' ? 'admin' : (user?.role || fallbackRole)),
        currentRole: (user?.email?.toLowerCase() === 'cjkishore35@gmail.com' && !user?.activeRole ? 'admin' : (user?.activeRole || user?.role || fallbackRole)),
        language,
        t: currentT,
        setLanguage,
        setUser,
        loginWithGoogle,
        loginWithOtp,
        requestOtp,
        switchRole,
        logout,
        isAuthModalOpen,
        setIsAuthModalOpen,
        isLoadingSession,
        sessionError,
        retrySession,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

==================== FILE: src\context\QueueContext.tsx ====================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { Booking, NotificationItem } from '../types';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from './AuthContext';

interface QueueContextType {
  activeBooking: Booking | null;
  notifications: NotificationItem[];
  unreadCount: number;
  refreshActiveBooking: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshNotifications: () => Promise<void>;
  markAllNotificationsRead: () => Promise<void>;
  cancelCurrentBooking: (reason?: string) => Promise<boolean | { error: string }>;
  notifyArriving: () => Promise<boolean>;
  setActiveBooking: (booking: Booking | null) => void;
  toastMessage: { title: string; message: string; type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' } | null;
  setToastMessage: (msg: { title: string; message: string; type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' } | null) => void;
}

const QueueContext = createContext<QueueContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const QueueProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [activeBooking, setActiveBooking] = useState<Booking | null>(null);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([]);
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<{
    title: string;
    message: string;
    type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert';
  } | null>(null);

  const prevPositionRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  // Play subtle web audio chime on notifications
  const playAlertChime = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AudioContextClass) return;
      const ctx = new AudioContextClass();
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();

      osc.type = 'sine';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(587.33, ctx.currentTime); // D5
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime + 0.15); // A5

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, ctx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.35);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);

      osc.start();
      osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.35);
      osc.onended = () => {
        void ctx.close().catch(() => {});
      };
    } catch (e) {
      // Audio might be blocked by browser policy until interaction
    }
  }, []);

  const refreshActiveBooking = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!user) {
      setActiveBooking(null);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await api.getBookings();
      if (res && res.bookings) {
        const now = new Date();
        const year = now.getFullYear();
        const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
        const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
        const todayLocal = `${year}-${month}-${day}`;

        // Find most recent active booking / token
        const active = res.bookings.find(
          (b) =>
            (!b.scheduledDate || b.scheduledDate >= todayLocal) &&
            (b.status === 'confirmed' || b.status === 'checked_in' || b.status === 'in_progress')
        );

        if (active) {
          // If queue position changed, trigger toast
          if (
            prevPositionRef.current !== null &&
            active.queuePosition !== undefined &&
            active.queuePosition !== prevPositionRef.current
          ) {
            if (active.queuePosition === 1) {
              setToastMessage({
                title: "You're Next! 🎉",
                message: `Your turn is up at ${active.salonName}. Head towards the chair!`,
                type: 'alert',
              });
              playAlertChime();
            } else if (active.queuePosition < prevPositionRef.current) {
              setToastMessage({
                title: 'Queue Moved Up!',
                message: `You are now #${active.queuePosition} in line. Est. wait: ~${active.estimatedWaitMinutes || 10}m.`,
                type: 'info',
              });
            }
          }

          prevPositionRef.current = active.queuePosition ?? null;
          setActiveBooking(active);
        } else {
          setActiveBooking(null);
          prevPositionRef.current = null;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error refreshing active booking:', e);
    }
  }, [user, playAlertChime]);

  const refreshNotifications = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!user) {
      setNotifications([]);
      return;
    }
    try {
      const res = await api.getNotifications();
      if (res && res.notifications) {
        setNotifications(res.notifications);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error fetching notifications:', e);
    }
  }, [user]);

  // Live polling every 15 seconds for real-time queue synchronization (avoids 429)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    refreshActiveBooking();
    refreshNotifications();

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.hidden) return;
      refreshActiveBooking();
      refreshNotifications();
    }, 15000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [user, refreshActiveBooking, refreshNotifications]);

  const markAllNotificationsRead = async () => {
    if (!user) return;
    try {
      await api.markAllNotificationsRead();
      setNotifications((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, read: true })));
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error marking notifications read:', e);
    }
  };

  const cancelCurrentBooking = async (reason?: string) => {
    if (!activeBooking) return false;
    try {
      const res = await api.cancelBooking(activeBooking.id, reason);
      if (res.success) {
        setActiveBooking(null);
        setToastMessage({
          title: 'Booking Cancelled',
          message: (res as any).refund?.refundAmount
            ? `Booking cancelled. Refund of ₹${(res as any).refund.refundAmount} initiated (${(res as any).refund.refundPercentage}%).`
            : 'Your booking was cancelled.',
          type: 'info',
        });
        refreshActiveBooking();
        return true;
      }
      if ((res as any).error) return { error: (res as any).error };
      return false;
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error('Error cancelling booking:', e);
      return { error: e?.message || 'Error cancelling booking' };
    }
  };

  const notifyArriving = async () => {
    if (!activeBooking) return false;
    try {
      const res = await api.markArriving(activeBooking.id);
      if (res.success) {
        setActiveBooking(res.booking);
        setToastMessage({
          title: 'Shop Notified!',
          message: `${activeBooking.salonName} knows you are on your way.`,
          type: 'success',
        });
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error sending arrival alert:', e);
      return false;
    }
  };

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length;

  return (
    <QueueContext.Provider
      value={{
        activeBooking,
        notifications,
        unreadCount,
        refreshActiveBooking,
        refreshNotifications,
        markAllNotificationsRead,
        cancelCurrentBooking,
        notifyArriving,
        setActiveBooking,
        toastMessage,
        setToastMessage,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </QueueContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useQueue = () => {
  const context = useContext(QueueContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useQueue must be used within a QueueProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

==================== FILE: src\utils\paymentBreakdown.ts ====================

/**
 * Single source of truth for payment breakdown math across customer UI, owner UI, and server.
 * All intermediate math is done in integer paise to avoid IEEE-754 floating point drift.
 */

export const QGO_FEE = 30;
export const GST_RATE = 0.18;
export const QGO_GST = 5.40;
export const QGO_CHARGES = 35.40;
export const MIN_ADVANCE = 100;

export const QGO_FEE_PAISE = 3000; // ₹30.00 in paise
export const QGO_GST_PAISE = 540;   // ₹5.40 in paise (18% of ₹30)
export const QGO_CHARGES_PAISE = 3540; // ₹35.40 in paise
export const MIN_ADVANCE_PAISE = 10000; // ₹100.00 in paise

export type CanonicalPaymentStatus = 'paid' | 'partially_paid' | 'pay_at_shop' | 'pending' | 'refunded';

/**
 * Normalizes any legacy or new payment status value to a canonical lowercase representation.
 */
export function normalizePaymentStatus(status?: string | null): CanonicalPaymentStatus {
  if (!status) return 'pay_at_shop';
  const clean = String(status).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (clean === 'paid' || clean === 'fully_paid') return 'paid';
  if (clean === 'partially_paid' || clean === 'partial') return 'partially_paid';
  if (clean === 'pay_at_shop' || clean === 'unpaid' || clean === 'cash') return 'pay_at_shop';
  if (clean === 'pending') return 'pending';
  if (clean === 'refunded') return 'refunded';
  return 'pay_at_shop';
}

export type PaymentBreakdownMode = 'ADVANCE' | 'FULL';

export interface PaymentBreakdownInput {
  slotTotal: number;
  advanceAmount?: number;
  mode: PaymentBreakdownMode;
}

export interface PaymentBreakdown {
  slotTotal: number;
  qgoFee: number;
  gst: number;
  payNow: number;
  slotCredit: number;
  balanceAtVenue: number;
}

/**
 * Computes exact payment breakdown for a booking.
 * 
 * In 'ADVANCE' mode:
 * - slotCredit: Net amount credited toward the slot (the configured advance amount).
 * - payNow: Total customer payment online = slotCredit + QGO_CHARGES (QGo convenience fee + GST).
 * - balanceAtVenue: Remaining amount customer pays at the venue = slotTotal - slotCredit.
 * 
 * In 'FULL' mode:
 * - slotCredit: Full slot total.
 * - payNow: slotTotal + QGO_CHARGES.
 * - balanceAtVenue: 0.
 * 
 * Invariant: payNow - qgoFee - gst + balanceAtVenue === slotTotal always holds.
 */
export function computeBreakdown({
  slotTotal,
  advanceAmount,
  mode,
}: PaymentBreakdownInput): PaymentBreakdown {
  const slotTotalPaise = Math.round(Number(slotTotal || 0) * 100);

  if (mode === 'FULL') {
    const payNowPaise = slotTotalPaise + QGO_CHARGES_PAISE;
    const slotCreditPaise = slotTotalPaise;
    const balanceAtVenuePaise = 0;

    return {
      slotTotal: Number((slotTotalPaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
      qgoFee: QGO_FEE,
      gst: QGO_GST,
      payNow: Number((payNowPaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
      slotCredit: Number((slotCreditPaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
      balanceAtVenue: Number((balanceAtVenuePaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
    };
  }

  // ADVANCE mode
  const advanceInputPaise = Math.round(Number(advanceAmount || 0) * 100);
  const slotCreditPaise = Math.min(slotTotalPaise, Math.max(0, advanceInputPaise));
  const payNowPaise = slotCreditPaise + QGO_CHARGES_PAISE;
  const balanceAtVenuePaise = Math.max(0, slotTotalPaise - slotCreditPaise);

  return {
    slotTotal: Number((slotTotalPaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
    qgoFee: QGO_FEE,
    gst: QGO_GST,
    payNow: Number((payNowPaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
    slotCredit: Number((slotCreditPaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
    balanceAtVenue: Number((balanceAtVenuePaise / 100).toFixed(2)),
  };
}

/**
 * Validates advance amount against slot price.
 * Returns null if valid, or an error string if invalid.
 */
export function validateAdvanceAmount(
  advance: number | undefined | null,
  slotPrice: number
): string | null {
  if (advance === undefined || advance === null || isNaN(advance)) {
    return 'Advance amount is required.';
  }
  if (advance < MIN_ADVANCE) {
    return `Advance must be at least ₹${MIN_ADVANCE}.`;
  }
  if (advance > slotPrice) {
    return `Advance cannot exceed slot price (₹${slotPrice}).`;
  }
  return null;
}

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Salon, Booking, GeneratedTurfSlot } from '../../types';
import { api } from '../../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { fmt12, getBusinessTodayStr } from '../../utils/timeFormat';
import { normalizePaymentStatus } from '../../utils/paymentBreakdown';
import { LiquidTabBar } from '../common/LiquidTabBar';
import {
  Search,
  Filter,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  User,
  Phone,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
  AlertCircle,
  Eye,
  Check,
  ChevronRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  DollarSign,
  Ticket,
  Plus,
  Send,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface OwnerBookingsViewProps {
  salon: Salon;
  onRefreshOverview?: () => void;
}

export const OwnerBookingsView: React.FC<OwnerBookingsViewProps> = ({ salon, onRefreshOverview }) => {
  const { t } = useAuth();
  const [bookings, setBookings] = useState<Booking[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // Filters & Tabs
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'all' | 'upcoming' | 'today' | 'completed' | 'cancelled'>('all');
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [dateFilter, setDateFilter] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const [paymentFilter, setPaymentFilter] = useState('all');

  // Detail Modal
  const [selectedBooking, setSelectedBooking] = useState<Booking | null>(null);
  const [actionLoading, setActionLoading] = useState(false);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState('');
  const [isManualBookingOpen, setIsManualBookingOpen] = useState(false);

  const fetchBookings = async (showLoading = true) => {
    if (showLoading) setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.getBookings({ salonId: salon.id });
      if (res && res.bookings) {
        setBookings(res.bookings);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error fetching bookings:', e);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void fetchBookings();
    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (!document.hidden) void fetchBookings(false);
    }, 10000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
  }, [salon.id]);

  const todayStr = getBusinessTodayStr();

  // Filtering
  const filteredBookings = bookings.filter((b) => {
    const bDate = b.scheduledDate || b.createdAt.split('T')[0];

    // Tab filter
    if (activeTab === 'upcoming' && (b.status !== 'confirmed' || bDate < todayStr)) return false;
    if (activeTab === 'today' && bDate !== todayStr) return false;
    if (activeTab === 'completed' && b.status !== 'completed') return false;
    if (activeTab === 'cancelled' && b.status !== 'cancelled') return false;

    // Search filter
    if (search) {
      const q = search.toLowerCase();
      const matchName = b.customerName?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchPhone = b.customerPhone?.includes(q);
      const matchId = b.id?.toLowerCase().includes(q) || b.tokenNumber?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      if (!matchName && !matchPhone && !matchId) return false;
    }

    // Additional dropdown filters
    if (dateFilter && bDate !== dateFilter) return false;
    if (statusFilter !== 'all' && b.status !== statusFilter) return false;
    if (paymentFilter !== 'all' && normalizePaymentStatus(b.paymentStatus) !== paymentFilter) return false;

    return true;
  });

  const handleUpdateStatus = async (bookingId: string, status: string) => {
    setActionLoading(true);
    setActionError('');
    try {
      await api.updateBookingStatus(bookingId, status);
      await fetchBookings();
      if (onRefreshOverview) onRefreshOverview();
      if (selectedBooking && selectedBooking.id === bookingId) {
        const updated = await api.getBooking(bookingId);
        if (updated?.booking) setSelectedBooking(updated.booking);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setActionError(e?.message || 'Error updating status');
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCollectBalance = async (bookingId: string) => {
    setActionLoading(true);
    setActionError('');
    try {
      await api.collectBalance(bookingId);
      await fetchBookings();
      if (onRefreshOverview) onRefreshOverview();
      if (selectedBooking && selectedBooking.id === bookingId) {
        const updated = await api.getBooking(bookingId);
        if (updated?.booking) setSelectedBooking(updated.booking);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setActionError(e?.message || 'Error collecting balance');
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCancelWithRefund = async (bookingId: string) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Are you sure you want to cancel this booking and process refund?')) return;
    setActionLoading(true);
    setActionError('');
    try {
      await api.cancelBooking(bookingId, 'Owner cancelled booking from management dashboard');
      await fetchBookings();
      if (onRefreshOverview) onRefreshOverview();
      setSelectedBooking(null);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setActionError(e?.message || 'Failed to cancel booking');
    } finally {
      setActionLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSendTicket = async (booking: Booking) => {
    const ticketText = [
      `QGo booking ticket — ${salon.name}`,
      `Booking ID: QGO-${booking.id.split('_').pop()?.toUpperCase()}`,
      `Customer: ${booking.customerName}`,
      `Slot: ${booking.scheduledDate || 'Today'} ${booking.scheduledTime ? `${fmt12(booking.scheduledTime)}${booking.slotEndTime ? ` - ${fmt12(booking.slotEndTime)}` : ''}` : ''}`,
      `Amount due at venue: ₹${booking.balanceAmount ?? booking.totalAmount ?? booking.amount}`,
      'GST: ₹0 | QGo convenience fee: ₹0',
      'Please show this ticket at the venue for check-in.',
    ].join('\n');
    try {
      if (navigator.share) await navigator.share({ title: `${salon.name} booking ticket`, text: ticketText });
      else {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(ticketText);
        window.alert('Ticket details copied. Send them to the customer by WhatsApp or SMS.');
      }
    } catch (shareError: any) {
      if (shareError?.name !== 'AbortError') setActionError('Could not share the ticket. Please try again.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      {/* ── Top Header ── */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#000000] tracking-tight">Bookings</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#444444]">Manage customer slots, check-ins, and tickets</p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setIsManualBookingOpen(true)}
          className="shrink-0 rounded-xl bg-[#E73229] px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition-transform active:scale-95"
        >
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Manual Booking</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ── 1. TABS & SEARCH BAR (LIQUID GLASS) ── */}
      <div className="glass-card p-3 sm:p-4 space-y-3">
        {/* Navigation Sub-Tabs with Liquid Gliding Indicator */}
        <div className="flex overflow-x-auto scrollbar-none w-full">
          <LiquidTabBar
            tabs={[
              { id: 'all', label: 'All Bookings' },
              { id: 'upcoming', label: 'Upcoming' },
              { id: 'today', label: 'Today' },
              { id: 'completed', label: 'Completed' },
              { id: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled' },
            ]}
            activeTab={activeTab}
            onChange={(tabId) => setActiveTab(tabId as any)}
            className="glass w-max max-w-full"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Search & Secondary Filters */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
          {/* Search */}
          <div className="sm:col-span-2 relative">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-[#444444] absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search customer, ID, phone..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl glass-input text-[14px] text-[#000000] font-medium placeholder-[#666666] focus:outline-none min-h-[42px]"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Date Picker */}
          <div>
            <input
              type="date"
              value={dateFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setDateFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl glass-input text-[14px] text-[#000000] font-medium focus:outline-none min-h-[42px]"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Status Dropdown */}
          <div>
            <select
              value={statusFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl glass-input text-[14px] text-[#000000] font-medium focus:outline-none min-h-[42px]"
            >
              <option value="all">All Statuses</option>
              <option value="confirmed">Confirmed</option>
              <option value="checked_in">Checked In</option>
              <option value="completed">Completed</option>
              <option value="cancelled">Cancelled</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── 2. BOOKINGS LIST ── */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="glass-card p-12 text-center text-[#444444]">
          <div className="w-7 h-7 border-2 border-red-500/30 border-t-[#E73229] rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-2" />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#000000]">Loading bookings...</p>
        </div>
      ) : filteredBookings.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="glass-card p-12 text-center text-[#444444] space-y-2">

          <p className="text-xs text-[#555555] max-w-sm mx-auto">
            Bookings will appear here when customers book your slots or services.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {filteredBookings.map((b) => {
            const isCompleted = b.status === 'completed';
            const isCancelled = b.status === 'cancelled';
            const isCheckedIn = b.status === 'checked_in';
            const shortId = `QGO-${b.id.split('_').pop()?.toUpperCase()}`;

            return (

                className="glass-card p-3 sm:p-4 cursor-pointer flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3 group min-w-0 hover:border-red-300 transition-all duration-150 active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 min-w-0">

                          isCompleted
                            ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200'
                            : isCancelled
                            ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-200'

                            ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border-blue-200'
                            : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {b.status}
                      </span>

                    <h3 className="font-bold text-[15px] text-[#000000] group-hover:text-red-600 transition-colors truncate">

                  </div>

                        ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border-rose-200 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-300 dark:border-rose-800'

              <div className="p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white/70 dark:bg-white/5 border border-white/80 dark:border-white/10 space-y-2.5 text-[13px] shadow-xs">

                  </span>

                  </span>
